What we are witnessing is a capriccious use of annexation designed to create a regulatory blind spot, effectively allowing mega corporations [clears throat] to bypass the oversight of the Wyoming Department of Environmental Qualities Industrial Sighting Council, ISC. Under Wyoming [02:35:42] state law, massive industrial projects that exceed specific cost and infrastructure thresholds are required to go through the ISC. This is a vital regulatory safeguard that's been in place since 1975. It's designed to [02:35:54] evaluate the socioeconomic impacts of me mega projects, analyze environmental footprints, and most importantly, mandate strict mitigation fees and community benefit agreements to protect local governments and taxpayers. Every [02:36:08] wind or solar project must go through this process. Why are data centers different? When a project is swallowed up into the city via massive single property annexation, developers are shielded, exempt from ISC review. This [02:36:22] is intentional. By rushing to annex nearly 3,500 acres of agricultural land, this council is actively helping multi-billion dollar developers evade the very state agency equipped to regulate them. Cheyenne City government is simply not staffed, structured, or funded to act as an industrial regulatory body. By approving this annexation without a fully realized master plan, you're abdicating your duty to protect the public. You're giving up all of our leverage.
